# META
~~~ini
description=Scoped collection expression bodies preserve parameters, local members, aliases and bare calc returns; evaluation remains explicit when higher-order execution is unavailable.
type=file
libraries=standard
require_no_diagnostics=true
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package CollectionBodies {
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import NumericalFunctions::*;
    attribute def Contribution {
        attribute included : Boolean;
        attribute amount : Real;
    }
    calc def Total {
        in contributions : Contribution[0..*];
        in value : Real;
        alias InputType for Contribution;
        return result : Real = sum(contributions
            ->select { in value : Contribution; value.included }
            ->collect { in value : Contribution;
                private attribute doubled : Real = value.amount * 2;
                doubled
            });
    }
    calc def BareReturn {
        in contributions : Real[0..*];
        return sum(contributions->select { in value : Real; value > 0.0 });
    }
    calc def DotShorthand {
        in contributions : Real[0..*];
        return result : Real = sum(contributions.?{ in value : Real; value > 0.0 });
    }
    calc def BodyMembers {
        return result : Real = { private attribute local : Real = 2.0; local };
    }
    constraint def AllPositive {
        in contributions : Real[0..*];
        contributions->forAll { in value : Real; value > 0.0 }
    }
}
~~~
